PASS sandbox-paths-callable -- VFS primitives return non-nil paths
PASS home-is-documents -- HOME equals ios-documents-directory
PASS cwd-is-documents -- Current working directory is the documents tree
PASS file-roundtrip -- find-file / write / re-read round-trips through Documents
PASS clipboard-roundtrip -- Lisp -> UIPasteboard -> Lisp preserves text
PASS display-geometry -- x-display-pixel-{width,height} report a real display
PASS appearance-callable -- ios-system-appearance returns dark or light
PASS image-load-png -- create-image on a 1x1 PNG returns a usable image object
PASS face-color-roundtrip -- set-face-attribute followed by face-attribute returns input
PASS frame-bg-mode-set -- frame-background-mode is one of light / dark
PASS drag-n-drop-handler-bound -- [drag-n-drop] is bound globally to a callable
